## DLC_ASTRAL_PLANES_RECOMMENDATION_DESC

## DLC_ASTRAL_PLANES_RECOMMENDATION_REC_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_ASTRAL_PLANES_RECOMMENDATION_REC_1&lang=en)

### 中文

## action_summon_dimensional_fleet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_summon_dimensional_fleet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与星界位面的调谐加深了。现在可以召唤异次元体战士来支援我们的事业。至于他们从何、为何而来，目前尚不得而知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:40`

### English

Our attunement to the astral planes has deepened. We are now able to call upon extradimensional warriors to support us in our cause. From where they come, or why they come, we cannot be sure.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:48`

## action_summon_dimensional_fleet_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_summon_dimensional_fleet_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

从一个未知的时空召唤一支强大的舰队。他们可以在我们的次元停留至多10年。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

Summon a powerful fleet from a place and time unknown. They can remain in our dimension for up to 10 years.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:49`

星界裂分

### English

Astral Splitting

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:56`

## action_astral_splitting_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_astral_splitting_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过将更多的$ASTRAL_THREADS_ICON$编织成空间的纤细织构，我们可以人为创造一处现实的裂隙。如果过于频繁尝试会导致星系陷入四分五裂的危险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

By weaving extra $ASTRAL_THREADS_ICON$ into the delicate fabric of space, we should be able to create an artificial tear in reality. Doing this too often would put the galaxy in danger of unravelling entirely.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:57`

## action_astral_splitting_no_rift_spawning_location

## action_flash_forge_hyper_relay

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_flash_forge_hyper_relay&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

Flash Forge Hyper Relay

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:84`

## action_flash_forge_hyper_relay_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_flash_forge_hyper_relay_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间航道本身具有有趣的量子性质，但通过将其与特定数量的星界丝交织，我们可以获得绝妙的结果。在突破其他的次元后，我们便可以取用现存（或未来将存在）的超空间中继器。几乎可以肯定，我们是唯一能行如此“借用”之事的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

Hyperlanes already possess interesting quantum properties, but by interweaving a given amount of astral thread, we achieved fantastic results. By breaking through to other dimensions, we can now access Hyper Relays that already exist (or will exist in the future.) We are nearly certain that we are the only ones 'borrowing' them in this manner.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:85`

## action_flash_forge_hyper_relay_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_flash_forge_hyper_relay_tooltip&lang=en)

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_dimensional_shifting_tooltip&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:93`

## action_planar_mastery_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_planar_mastery_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

前往星界位面的旅程让我们对维系宇宙的织构有了更深的了解。这些丝线几乎没有其他人能理解，我们却能以其尽情编织。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

Our journeys into the Astral Planes have helped us better understand the fabric that binds the universes. We can weave these threads in ways few others comprehend.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:118`

### 中文

在对§Y[From.GetStarName]§!进行调查时，[Root.GetName]发现了一种新的战略资源，称为§H$astral_threads$§!。它在扭曲时空方面的独特性质可以用在若干有趣的用途。

## astral_planes.100.desc.split

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.100.desc.split&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.solar_system.GetName]星系的细缝发生了失稳，打开了一道通往其他时空的裂隙。\n\n我们应当小心应对。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

The dimensional scar in the [From.solar_system.GetName] system has destabilized, opening a rift into another place and time.\n\nWe should approach with caution.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:164`

## astral_planes.100.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.100.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[from.solar_system.GetName]星系发现了一处特别的时空裂隙。也许我们花时间研究一些其特质就可以找到进一步探索的方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

We have discovered an extraordinary rift in space and time in the [from.solar_system.GetName] system. Perhaps if we take time to study its properties we can find a way to explore further.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:165`

### English

## astral_planes.101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的人终于抵近到触手可及天空中裂缝的地方了。\n\n也许花点时间研究其性质便可以找到进一步探索的方法。我们完全无从得知在它里面到底有什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

Our people have finally reached far enough to touch the scar in our skies.\n\nPerhaps if we take time to study its properties, we can find a way to explore further. We have no way of knowing what may be on the other side.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:168`

## astral_planes.101.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.101.a&lang=en)

我们已经达成了先祖不曾触及之事。

### English

We have done what our ancestors could not.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:169`

## astral_planes.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经揭开了关于如何收集储存这种构成我们宇宙边界的星界物质形成的缥缈丝线的秘密。\n\n这种珍贵稀有的资源可以以诸多方式使用，使我们大受裨益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

We have unlocked the secrets behind gathering and containing the volatile threads of astral material that make up the very border of this universe.\n\nThis valuable and rare resource can be applied in various ways that will benefit us greatly.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:171`

## astral_planes.120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们失去了对[FromFrom.GetName]星系的控制，一并也失去了其中所含的时空裂隙。\n\n我们进一步探索的努力只能暂时告一段落了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

We have lost control of the [FromFrom.GetName] system, and the rift in space and time contained within.\n\nOur efforts to reach beyond have been abandoned for now.

## situation_a_rift_in_space_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_a_rift_in_space_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一处令人惊奇的时空裂隙。如果花时间研究其性质，我们或许能找到进一步探索的方式。我们无从得知里面究竟有什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:150`

### English

We have discovered an extraordinary rift in space and time. Perhaps if we take time to study its properties, we can find a way to explore further. We have no way of knowing what may be on the other side.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:182`

## situation_a_rift_in_space_desc_riftworld_origin

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_a_rift_in_space_desc_riftworld_origin&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的人终于抵近到触手可及天空中裂缝的地方了。\n\n也许花点时间研究其性质便可以找到进一步探索的方法。我们完全无从得知在它里面到底有什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:151`

### English

Our people have finally reached far enough to touch the scar in our skies.\n\nPerhaps if we take time to study its properties, we can find a way to explore further. We have no way of knowing what may be on the other side.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:183`

可以让我们的科学家们分配大量的研究资源以揭开这一时空裂隙的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

## approach_situation_a_rift_in_space_send_probes_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aapproach_situation_a_rift_in_space_send_probes_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

往裂隙中发送的每一个探测器都能加深我们对如何穿透其边界的理解。我们可以分配合金总产出的一部分用于此项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

Each probe we send into the rift furthers our understanding of how to penetrate its borders. We are able to dedicate a certain amount of our total Alloy production for this purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:189`

## astral_rift_discovery_event_text

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift_discovery_event_text&lang=en)

### 中文

在研究§Y[target.solar_system.GetName]§!中的星界裂隙时，我们不可避免地扰动了这个维度异常，造成了严重的时空扭曲，让附近的现实以不可预知的方式发生了歪斜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

While studying the Astral Rift in §Y[target.solar_system.GetName]§!, we have inadvertently destabilized the dimensional anomaly. This is causing violent distortions in space and time, bending nearby reality in an unpredictable manner.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:195`

## astral_situation.55.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.55.name&lang=en)

$astral_rift_discovery_event_title$

### English

$astral_rift_discovery_event_title$

## astral_situation.55.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.55.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift_discovery_event_text$\n\n一颗我们称为§Y[planet_rift_in_space.GetName]§!的神秘行星突然无中生有般出现。我们任何一张星图中都没有其此前存在的记载。\n\n其上承载着一个[GetPreFTLLower]文明。\n\n我们只能推测它是通过某种方式来自我们宇宙之外的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

$astral_rift_discovery_event_text$\n\nA mysterious planet, which we are calling §Y[planet_rift_in_space.GetName]§!, has appeared where before there was nothing. We have no records of its previous existence on any of our star charts.\n\nIt is populated by a [GetPreFTLLower] civilization.\n\nOur best guess is that it somehow arrived from outside of our universe.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:197`

## astral_situation.60.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.60.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift_discovery_event_title$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

$astral_rift_discovery_event_title$

## astral_situation.60.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.60.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift_discovery_event_text$\n\n在§Y[rift_temporary_species_planet.GetName]§!凭空出现了一个感性物种，均匀地与我们现存的人口混居着。\n\n[rift_temporary_species.GetName]并没有关于他们来自何方的记忆，且没有表现出任何敌意。\n\n无论他们来自哪里，看来我们都没有办法帮他们回家了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

$astral_rift_discovery_event_text$\n\nA new sentient species has materialized on §Y[rift_temporary_species_planet.GetName]§!, distributed evenly across our existing population.\n\nThe [rift_temporary_species.GetName] have no memory of where they are from and do not appear hostile.\n\nWherever they originated, it is unlikely we can help them return.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:199`

## astral_situation.65.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.65.name&lang=en)

### English

$astral_rift_discovery_event_title$

## astral_situation.65.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.65.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift_discovery_event_text$\n\n§Y[rift_temporary_planet.GetName]§!上严重的扭曲导致时间以两倍速流逝。我们需要密切关注这一局势，确保翻倍供应补给。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

$astral_rift_discovery_event_text$\n\nSignificant temporal distortions on §Y[rift_temporary_planet.GetName]§! are causing time to pass twice as quickly. We should monitor the situation closely, and make sure to double their supplies.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:201`

### English

## rift_in_space_time_acceleration_modifier_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arift_in_space_time_acceleration_modifier_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星上的时间流逝速度翻倍了。

### English

Time passes twice as quickly on this world.

## astral_situation.20.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.20.name&lang=en)

### 中文

摩擦问题

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

Friction Is the Problem

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:206`

## astral_situation.20.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.20.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现，想要钻进时空的折缝中需要完美光滑的船体。\n\n只要分配足够的研究资源和时间，我们确信自己能研发这种装置，并可以暂时将其命名为“§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!”。然后我们就有可能发现这个空间异常背后的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

We have discovered that slipping through the folds in space and time requires a perfectly smooth vessel.\n\nWe are certain that we can develop such a device, what we are tentatively calling a "§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!", should we devote enough research and time. We may then finally discover what is beyond this spatial anomaly.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:207`

## astral_planes.2005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$tech_rift_sphere$科技已开发

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

$tech_rift_sphere$ Technology Developed

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:209`

## astral_planes.2005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经开发出了这种能在星界裂隙的折缝中穿行的科技：§Y$tech_rift_sphere_plural$§!。\n\n这种球体以高度先进、绝对光滑的纳米材料建成，可以在时空的乱流处无摩擦地滑行。\n\n因为不知道所到地方的情况，每一艘§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!都会连接上一条锚定缆线。缆线连接在我们宇宙中的一艘科研船上，以便在发生意外时将我们拉回。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

We have developed technology that will allow us to slip between the folds of an Astral Rift: §Y$tech_rift_sphere_plural$§!.\n\nConstructed of a highly advanced, perfectly smooth nano-material, the Sphere is able to frictionlessly glide through turbulent areas of space and time.\n\nAs we are unsure where we will arrive, each §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! is connected to an anchor cable. To pull us back in case of emergency, the cable is tethered to a Science Ship in our universe.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:210`

## astral_planes.2005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那就看看能找到什么吧……

### English

Let's see what we can find...

## tech_rift_sphere_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_rift_sphere_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎时空倾向于汇聚成一层层互相叠盖的位面。在折缝中滑行的技巧则是关键。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:180`

### English

## RIFT_DIFFICULTY_TYPE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARIFT_DIFFICULTY_TYPE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

星界裂隙的每一阶段的探索难度水平可能存在差异。\n$RIFT_DIFFICULTY_EASY$阶段的难度为0-2，$RIFT_DIFFICULTY_NORMAL$为3-5，而$RIFT_DIFFICULTY_HARD$为6以上。难度越高，探索时段结束时成功结果的出现概率就越低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Each chapter in an Astral Rift can have a different difficulty value.\n$RIFT_DIFFICULTY_EASY$ chapters are 0-2, $RIFT_DIFFICULTY_NORMAL$ 3-5, and $RIFT_DIFFICULTY_HARD$ 6+. Higher difficulty may reduce the likelihood of a successful result at the end of an exploration phase.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:264`

§H探索时段§!\n星界裂隙的探索将会按时段进行，在每个时段结束会掷一次进度骰子。\n\n下一个时段将会在£time£$DAYS|Y$后结束

## EXPLORATION_LOG_ROLL_TOOLTIP

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AEXPLORATION_LOG_ROLL_TOOLTIP&lang=en)

### 中文

§H探索时段结果§!\n已收集线索：$GAINED|Y$\n\n结果：$RESULT|Y$\n  投掷点数：$ROLL|Y$\n  线索数：$CLUES|Y$\n  技能加成：$BONUS|Y$\n  难度值：$DIFFICULTY|R$\n\n§E最终结果：§!\n§R失败§!：结果值小于或等于§Y4§!，添加§Y0§!点线索。\n§Y取得进展§!：结果值介于§Y5-9§!之间，添加§Y1§!点线索。\n§G取得进展§!：结果值介于§Y10-13§!之间，添加§Y2§!点线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

§HPhase Results§!\nClues Gained: $GAINED|Y$\n\nResult: $RESULT|Y$\n  Roll: $ROLL|Y$\n  Clues: $CLUES|Y$\n  Skill Bonus: $BONUS|Y$\n  Difficulty: $DIFFICULTY|R$\n\n§ERoll Outcomes:§!\n§RFailure§!: Results §Y4§! or less add §Y0§! clues.\n§YProgress§!: Results between §Y5-9§! add §Y1§! clues.\n§GProgress§!: Results between §Y10-13§! add §Y2§! clues.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:279`

## astral_rift_roll_header

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift_roll_header&lang=en)

### 中文

§H$ASTRAL_RIFT_BREAKTHROUGH$§!\n此数值反映你的科学官在探索当前裂隙时取得突破的几率。\n\n每当一个探索时段结束，游戏将自动掷出一枚骰子（结果范围为§Y1§!到§Y10§!）。掷骰结果会与§Y技能加成§!和§Y线索§!数相加，随后减去§Y难度§!值。\n\n掷骰前的结果是$SCORE|0Y$。\n\n§E最终结果：§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

§H$ASTRAL_RIFT_BREAKTHROUGH$§!\nThis reflects the chance of your Scientist making a breakthrough during exploration of the current rift.\n\nEvery time a phase ends, a die (§Y1§! to §Y10§!) is rolled. The result of the roll is added to §YSkill Bonus§! and §YClues§!, then subtracted by §YDifficulty§!.\n\nThe current score before dice roll is $SCORE|0Y$.\n\n§ERoll Outcomes:§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:281`

## warpling_army_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awarpling_army_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为战争而设计的超凡生物，这些全副武装的幼雏能够瞬间越过一小段空间，执行的包抄和伏击机动让敌人瞠目结舌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:256`

### English

Otherworldly creatures engineered for war, these armored hatchlings are able to instantly traverse small distances, allowing the execution of impressive flanking and ambush maneuvers.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:311`

## flamestorm_troopers_army_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aflamestorm_troopers_army_desc&lang=en)

## astral_planes.210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.210.name&lang=en)

### English

Scientist Returns

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:316`

## astral_planes.210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[fromfrom.leader.GetName]从一趟寻找因终末而消失的星系的$ASTRAL_RIFT$探险中返回了。他熟悉的许多地方已经完全从本现实中消失了。\n\n他还不确定自己回到的是不是正确的维度，显然是受到了一定的认知冲击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

[fromfrom.leader.GetName] returned from exploring an $ASTRAL_RIFT$ to find a galaxy lost to the End. Many of the places they knew have vanished completely from this existence.\n\nThey are still uncertain if they returned to the correct dimension at all, and appear to be experiencing a form of cognitive shock as a result.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:317`

## FLEETORDER_ASTRAL_JUMP_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFLEETORDER_ASTRAL_JUMP_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

通过折叠超空间，装配有跃迁引擎的舰队可以瞬间抵达所建疆域内的任何位置。每一次折叠需要有$COOLDOWN|Y$天的冷却，并消耗$COST|Y$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

By folding hyperspace, Jump Drive-equipped fleets can instantly Jump anywhere within established borders. Each instance of folding has a cooldown of $COOLDOWN|Y$ days and requires $COST|Y$.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:353`

## FLEETORDER_PHASE_FLEET_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFLEETORDER_PHASE_FLEET_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

军用舰队可相位转移离开我们的现实位面。相移舰队将会在$DURATION|Y$天后返回——并被完全维修。每一支舰队只能相隔$COOLDOWN|Y$天执行相位转移，且需要$COST|Y$。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

Military fleets can phase out of our plane of reality. Phased fleets will return in $DURATION|Y$ days - fully repaired. Each fleet can only be Phased Out once every $COOLDOWN|Y$ days, and requires $COST|Y$.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:359`

## action_dimensional_lock_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_dimensional_lock_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

使科研船能在虫洞、L-星门或者虚境隧道放置一枚$DIMENSIONAL_LOCK$。一旦上锁，只有获得你许可的舰队才可进入。$DIMENSIONAL_LOCKS$将维持§Y10§!年。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

Allow your Science Ships to place one $DIMENSIONAL_LOCK$ on a Wormhole, L-Gate, or Shroud Tunnel. Once locked, only those fleets with your permission may enter. $DIMENSIONAL_LOCKS$ last for §Y10§! years.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:371`

## action_dimensional_lock_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction_dimensional_lock_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过将我们的舰船与一处空间折叠调谐至完全一致的量子特征，我们便可以使它的频率与我们平行。然而，这样的调谐不是永久的。这种缺口将会逐渐落回其自然的趋势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

By attuning our ships to the unique quantum signatures of a fold in space, we can align its frequencies with our own. However, such an attunement is not permanent. Eventually, the breach will gravitate back toward its natural inclination.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:372`

### English

## origin_riftworld_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_riftworld_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一道巨大的时空裂隙高悬于该文明母星的穹顶。在研究这一现象近千年后，最近的星舰科技进展使其终于可以靠近他，并且发掘潜藏其中的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

A massive rift in space and time looms near this civilization's homeworld. After studying the phenomenon for millennia, recent advances in starship technology will allow them to finally reach out and discover what secrets it might hold.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:446`

## origin_tooltip_riftworld_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_tooltip_riftworld_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—$HOMEWORLD$['concept_riftworld_digsite']['concept_archaeology_site']$NEW_LINE$—有[ 'concept_astral_rift',$ASTRAL_RIFTS$]的恒星系内的殖民行星：['modifier:riftworld_affinity']$NEW_LINE$—在你控制的恒星系内出现§Y$ASTRAL_RIFTS$§!的概率：§G+100%§!\n—$science_plural$可以探索§Y未拥有星系§!中的§Y$ASTRAL_RIFTS$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

- $HOMEWORLD$ ['concept_riftworld_digsite'] ['concept_archaeology_site']$NEW_LINE$- Colonized Planets in systems with [ 'concept_astral_rift',$ASTRAL_RIFTS$]: ['modifier:riftworld_affinity']$NEW_LINE$- Chance for §Y$ASTRAL_RIFTS$§! to appear in systems you control: §G+100%§!\n- $science_plural$ can explore §Y$ASTRAL_RIFTS$§! in §Yunowned Systems§!.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:447`

## START_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD&lang=en)

### 中文

自我们有记忆起，大裂隙就在[GetHomeWorldName]的天空中高悬着。传说我们的遥远过去与这道天界之痕还有我们首都附近的天降之坑相互纠缠。\n\n我们祖祖辈辈都将这个撞击坑视为圣地，却对其知之甚少。然而，最近的地震活动却让坑下的一处异常现象重见天日。我们正在着手向其核心钻探。至于在坑里到底会有什么发现，人们莫衷一是。$START_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_END$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

For as long as we can remember, the Great Rift has loomed in the skies above [GetHomeWorldName]. Legends propose that our past is intimately entwined with this mysterious celestial scar and an ancient impact site near our capital.\n\nFor generations, the site was revered, but little understood. However, recent seismic activity has uncovered subsurface anomalies in the crater. Preparations are already underway to drill into its core. Opinions remain divided on what we may uncover there.$START_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_END$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:448`

## START_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_MACHINE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_MACHINE&lang=en)

### 中文

穷尽我们数据库所能追溯的最早时间，大裂隙就早已高悬于[GetHomeWorldName]的上空。我们的内部时钟于该裂隙天体的频率相同步，而最早的记录则记录了一处位于我们首都附近的远古撞击坑。\n\n无数次迭代以来，该撞击坑都被保留下来，但却对其几乎没有任何了解。然而，最近的地震活动却让坑下的一处异常现象重见天日。我们正在准备向其核心钻探。$START_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_END$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

For as long as our databanks reach back, the Great Rift has loomed in the skies above [GetHomeWorldName]. Our internal clocks are synced with the frequency of the celestial scar and our earliest records describe an ancient impact site near our capital.\n\nFor many iterations, the site was left alone, but little understood. However, recent seismic activity has uncovered subsurface anomalies in the crater. Preparations are already underway to drill into its core.$START_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_END$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:449`

## START_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_END

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_END&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n这难道只是一个发生在我们超空间引擎科技取得进展时的普通巧合吗？就在我们直抵天界时，我们的注意力被重新拉回了地面上。也许在这颗星球的地下潜藏着的是对于我们前往星间旅行的启迪，也许它可以让我们做好应对裂隙另一端的事物的准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

\n\nIs it simple coincidence that this has occurred at the advent of Hyperdrive technology? Just as we reach for the heavens, our attention is pulled back to the ground. Perhaps what lies beneath the surface of this world is meant to aid us on our journey to the stars. Perhaps it can prepare us for whatever lies beyond the Rift.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:450`

## START_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_NOMADS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_RIFTWORLD_NOMADS&lang=en)

### 中文

自我们有记忆起，大裂隙就在虚空中高悬着——它就这样一直横亘在我们旅途的地平线之上，我们无法解释，却又无比熟悉。传说我们的遥远过去与这道天界之痕，以及深藏于我们方舟船体内部的某个远古撞击点有着千丝万缕的关系。\n\n多年以来，这个地方俨然成为圣地，却鲜有人能真正了解。然而，最近方舟核心系统出现的波动，将人们的注意力引向了撞击点附近那些长期封闭的舱室。我们正准备突破这些舱室。至于我们会在那里发现什么，大家众说纷纭。\n\n系统波动恰好发生在超光速引擎技术问世之际，这会是单纯的巧合吗？正当我们要伸手触碰星辰之时，注意力却被拉回了自己的飞船。或许，封存在我们方舟内部的东西，本就注定要助我们一臂之力。或许，它能让我们做好准备，去面对大裂隙另一侧可能存在的任何事物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

For as long as we can remember, the Great Rift has hung before us in the void - a constant presence on the horizon of our journey, as familiar as it is unexplained. Legends propose that our past is intimately entwined with this mysterious celestial scar, and with an ancient impact buried deep within the hull of our arkship.\n\nFor generations, the site was revered, but little understood. However, recent fluctuations in the arkship's core systems have drawn attention to long-sealed chambers near the impact point. Preparations are already underway to breach them. Opinions remain divided on what we may uncover there.\n\nIs it simple coincidence that this has occurred at the advent of Hyperdrive technology? Just as we reach for the stars, our attention is pulled back to our own vessel. Perhaps what lies sealed within our arkship is meant to aid us on our journey. Perhaps it can prepare us for whatever lies beyond the Rift.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:451`

## riftworld_affinity

## riftworld_affinity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ariftworld_affinity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的社会发展在跨维度存在的影响下，激发出对科学研究进行深化探索的热情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Our societal development under the influence of interdimensional forces drives a fervor for further research.

## astral_planes.2105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2105.name&lang=en)

怪异通讯

### English

Strange Transmission

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:456`

## astral_planes.2105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从§H[From.solar_system.GetName]§!内星界裂隙的爆炸余波中，我们收到了一段奇怪的通讯。\n\n[GetScientistPlural]们认为它源某个自远超裂隙本身的地方，且目前尚且只破译了其中的只言片语。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

In the aftermath of the exploration of the Astral Rift in §H[From.solar_system.GetName]§!, a mysterious transmission was received.\n\nOur [GetScientistPlural] have determined its origin to be from beyond the rift itself, and have only been able to translate a few words.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:457`

## astral_planes.2110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2110.desc&lang=en)

……与这个位面……同调……我们……会找到你……

### English

...attune... with the planes... we will find you...

## astral_planes.2120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现地震异常

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

Seismic Anomaly Detected

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:462`

## astral_planes.2120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

近期的地震活动意外地让一处毗邻我们首都的远古撞击坑显露了其庐山真面目。\n\n这座独一无二的坑洞一定与我们[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]物种的真正起源息息相关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

Recent seismic activity has revealed unexpected features of the ancient impact site near our capital.\n\nThis specific crater has been linked to our very origin as [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:463`

## riftworld_digsite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ariftworld_digsite&lang=en)

### 中文

远古撞击坑

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

Ancient Crater

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:465`

## riftworld_digsite_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ariftworld_digsite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[Planet.GetName]地表上的撞击坑的极深处探测到了异常的地震活动。地下声呐发现了一座前所未知的洞穴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

We've detected anomalous seismic activity from deep inside an ancient crater on the surface of [Planet.GetName]. Underground sonar revealed a previously unknown cavity.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:466`

## astral_planes.2125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

高速撞击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

High Speed Impact

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:467`

## astral_planes.2125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在撞击坑底部的初步发掘工作中，我们发现了一条深入地下深层的圆柱体撞击岩。\n\n这一岩层必然源自某种类陨石体的极高速撞击。初步计算表明，该物体几乎是以高亚光速发生的碰撞。它的重见天日宛若奇迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

Initial excavations into the base of the crater revealed a fully-formed cylinder of impactite lodged deep into the earth.\n\nThis vein must have originated from a meteoric impact of extremely high velocity. Initial calculations demonstrate the object must have arrived at a measurable fraction of the speed of light. It's a wonder that it made it to the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:468`

## astral_planes.2125.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2125.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续发掘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

Continue excavations.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:469`

## astral_planes.2130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

地下洞穴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

Underground Caverns

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:470`

## astral_planes.2130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在撞击岩层的终点，我们发现了一座豁然开朗的洞穴系统。\n\n地下水形成的水洼滋养着岩壁上灰暗纤弱的植被。在其中一个水池的地下，静静地躺着一个与众不同的球状物体。显然它不是自然生成的，而是这个撞击点的始作俑者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

At the termination point of the impactite vein, we have discovered an expansive cave system.\n\nPools of underground water support thin lines of pale vegetation along the cavern walls. At the bottom of one of these pools lies the remnants of an anomalous spherical object. It is not a natural formation, and appears to be the source of this impact site.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:471`

## astral_planes.2130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2130.a&lang=en)

### 中文

取回该物体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

Recover the object.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:472`

## astral_planes.2135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远古球体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

Ancient Sphere

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:473`

## astral_planes.2135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从地下湖中取回了那个球体。\n\n尽管被一些植被覆盖，其大部分仍保持得相当完整。其建造方式怪异地混杂着高度复杂的设计和粗糙的组装。完美光滑的金属板覆盖着舰船的前半部分，可以用来显著降低再入大气时的阻力。\n\n它所处位置的弹道回推与撞击岩处于一条直线上，由此可以确认该球体就是那颗把我们带到此地的类陨石体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

We have recovered the sphere from the underground lake.\n\nWhile covered in vegetative growth, much of it remains fully intact. Its construction is a strange mix of highly sophisticated design and crude assembly. Perfectly smooth metallic plates cover the frontal portion of the ship, which must have reduced friction significantly upon atmospheric entry.\n\nThe trajectory of its resting site aligns with the vein of impactite, confirming that this sphere is the source of the metamorphic rock that led us here. Even more incredibly, there are signs that this vessel had passengers.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:474`

## astral_planes.2140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

先祖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

Ancestors

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:475`

## astral_planes.2140.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2140.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在这个远古球体的内部取得了一箱档案文件，上面书写着一种我们语言的远古版本。提到的许多名字与我们最神圣的传说一一对应。\n\n盘根错节的梯道网络从撞击坑底一路蜿蜒崎岖地爬向一座巨大的隧道系统。从其中绳索纤维年代的跨度来看，这些隧道居住过数代人，这些幸存者们一点一点地凿开了通向地表的路。\n\n然而，目前为止的发现都无法让我们对最后一个发现做好心理准备：那是一座包含着一处坟地的洞穴。对其中的遗骸进行基因分析得出了令人惊讶的结果：这些人正是我们的祖先。\n\n至于他们又从何而来，为何此地被遗忘在了历史长河之中，又是新的谜团。抢救出来的档案保存状态不佳，但我们还是从球体中修复了一些数据日志。显然，这艘舰船是通过那道靠近[from.planet.GetName]的星界裂隙来到我们的恒星系的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

We have recovered a cache of documents from within the ancient sphere, each written in an archaic version of our own language. Several of these mention names associated with our most sacred legends.\n\nAn intricate network of ladders snakes upward from the impact site to a massive tunnel system. Based on the varying ages of the rope fibers, these caverns must have been occupied for several generations as the crash survivors worked their way toward the surface.\n\nHowever, nothing we'd discovered thus far could have prepared us for the final revelation: one of the upper chambers contained a burial site. DNA analysis conducted on the remains within revealed a shocking truth: these people are our ancestors.\n\nWhere exactly they came from, or why this place was forgotten, is yet unknown. The salvaged documents are in a poorly preserved state, but we have recovered a data log from the sphere. It clearly shows that the vessel arrived in our star system by means of the Astral Rift near [from.planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:476`

## astral_planes.2140.desc.has_rift_sphere

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2140.desc.has_rift_sphere&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_planes.2140.desc.common$\n\n尽管显然比我们的科技低级，这座球体正是其建造者渴望和才智的见证。这是一种逃离裂隙另一侧悲惨命运的终极手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

$astral_planes.2140.desc.common$\n\nWhile clearly less advanced than our own tech, this sphere is a testament to its builders desperation and ingenuity. This was a final effort to escape what must have been a terrible fate on the far side of the rift.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:477`

## astral_planes.2140.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2140.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_planes.2140.desc.common$\n\n通过一些研究，我们应该能够复制甚至改进用来建造这种具有跨越维度能力的船体的科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

$astral_planes.2140.desc.common$\n\nWith some study, we should be able to replicate and even improve upon the technology required to construct a vessel with these dimension-spanning capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:478`

## astral_planes.2140.desc.machine.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2140.desc.machine.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在远古球体中发现了一台存储器，这个储存器是由我们文件系统的某个上古版本进行编码的。\n\n盘根错节的梯道网络从撞击坑底一路蜿蜒崎岖地爬向一座巨大的隧道系统。从其中缆绳纤维年代的跨度来看，这些隧道曾经容纳过许多次迭代，这些撞击事件的幸存者们一点一点地凿开了通向地表的路。\n\n最靠上的一处遗址包含着一些与我们最早期型号的部件非常相似的东西。这些幸存者应该就是我们的先祖。\n\n至于他们又从何而来，为何此地被遗忘在了历史长河之中尚不得而知。我们从球体中取回的数据清楚地表明，抵达我们星系的这艘球状舰船正是来自[from.planet.GetName]附近的星界裂隙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

We have recovered a data cache from within the ancient sphere, a cache that was encoded in an archaic version of our file system.\n\nAn intricate network of ladders snakes upward from the impact site to a massive tunnel system. Based on the varying ages of the rope fibers, these caverns must have been occupied for several iterations as the crash survivors worked their way toward the surface.\n\nOne of the upper sites contained parts reminiscent of those used by our earliest models. These survivors were our predecessors.\n\nWhere exactly they came from, or why this location was lost, is yet unknown. The data long we recovered from the sphere clearly shows that the vessel arrived in our star system by means of the Astral Rift near [from.planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:479`

## astral_planes.2140.desc.machine.has_rift_sphere

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2140.desc.machine.has_rift_sphere&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_planes.2140.desc.machine.common$\n\n虽然显然没有我们自己的技术先进，但这个球体正是其建设者之绝望和创造力的见证。这是他们逃避裂隙彼端骇人命运的最后努力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

$astral_planes.2140.desc.machine.common$\n\nWhile clearly less advanced than our own tech, this sphere is a testament to its builders' desperation and ingenuity. This was a final effort to escape what must have been a terrible fate on the far side of the rift.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:480`

## astral_planes.2140.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.2140.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_planes.2140.desc.machine.common$\n\n通过一些研究，我们应该能够复制甚至改进用来建造这种具有跨越维度能力的船体的科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

$astral_planes.2140.desc.machine.common$\n\nWith some study, we should be able to replicate and even improve upon the technology required to construct a vessel with these dimension-spanning capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:481`

## gov_dimensional_covenant_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agov_dimensional_covenant_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该社会以一种深层的灵魂共识作为基石。他们施加严格的道德规范以确保最广大人民能笃信来世。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

## sm_dimensional_shrine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asm_dimensional_shrine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在已知的宇宙之外，存在通往其他世界的路径，那些同其他世界相互连接处的构造十分精妙。这就是我们建造最神圣的寺庙的地方。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Outside the known universe there are pathways to other worlds, and places where those connections are subtly made. This is where we build the holiest of our temples.\n

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:512`

## sm_reloading_bay_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asm_reloading_bay_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为我们的武器组件重新装填保留了恒星基地的重要部分，并提供额外的防御平台。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

Reserves a significant portion of the Starbase for reloading our weapon components, and provides for extra defense platforms.\n

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:514`

## civic_hyperspace_specialty

### 中文

超空间特长

### English

Hyperspace Specialty

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:517`

## civic_hyperspace_specialty_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_hyperspace_specialty_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

速度在该社会中是最受欢迎的。对超空间的操控不过是一种上升渠道，让那些在该领域最有天赋的人得以晋升到最高的阶层。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

This society has always embraced speed. The manipulation of hyperspace is just another thoroughfare, and those with aptitude in the field have risen to the highest caste.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:518`

## civic_hyperspace_specialty_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_hyperspace_specialty_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

必有研究选项 £physics£§Y$tech_hyper_drive_2$§!。\n拥有初始星系3跳超空间航道内的星系的低级情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

£physics£ §Y$tech_hyper_drive_2$§! is Guaranteed Research Option.\nHas Low Intel on all systems within 3 Hyperlanes of your starting system.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:519`

## civic_hive_hyperspace_specialty

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_hive_hyperspace_specialty&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间同调

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

Hyperspace Synchronicity

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:520`

## civic_hive_hyperspace_specialty_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_hive_hyperspace_specialty_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该集体追求速度。超空间操控的方法论深深地植根于整个集体之中，使其能够逐步理解距离-时间比的基本原理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

This collective seeks speed. The methodologies of hyperspace manipulation are embedded deep throughout the collective, enabling a progressive understanding of the fundamental principles of distance over time.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:521`

## civic_hive_hyperspace_specialty_effects

## civic_machine_hyperspace_specialty_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_machine_hyperspace_specialty_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个集合体追求速度。超空间操控的理论深深地蚀刻进入了每个子个体的处理单元中，使得集合体对距离-时间比的基本原则有着不断进步的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

This collective seeks speed. Theories of hyperspace manipulation are hard-wired into the processing unit of every drone, granting the collective a progressive understanding of the fundamental principles of distance over time.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:524`

## civic_corporate_hyperspace_specialty_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_corporate_hyperspace_specialty_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

时间就是金钱，而这个寰宇企业也非常重视速度。对研发的大量投资已经得到了丰厚的回报，使得该公司拥有了独家使用尖端超空间操纵技术的权限。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

Time is money, and this Megacorporation values speed. Heavy investment in R&D has paid off handsomely, granting the Corporation proprietary access to cutting-edge hyperspace manipulation techniques.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:527`

## civic_dimensional_worship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_dimensional_worship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个社会相信，在时空的褶皱之外有着神圣的存在。对他们来说，不稳定度最高的系统具有神圣的意义，既是朝圣地，也是研究的场所。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

This society believes that a divine existence lies beyond the folds in space and time. Systems where instabilities are highest hold a sacred meaning to them as sites of both pilgrimage and research.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:535`

## civic_sovereign_guardianship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_sovereign_guardianship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个由相互争斗的部落组成的集合，已经在长久的历史上形成了对固守领土和防御姿态的偏好。现在他们团结一致，准备将这种哲学带到星空中，旨在保持他们的边界稳固，并缓和紧张局势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

This collection of warring tribes has developed a historical preference for entrenched territories and defensive posturing. Now united, they are prepared to bring this philosophy to the stars, aiming to keep their borders solid and tensions low.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:561`

### 中文

这个由相互竞争的企业构成的社会已经认识到，对于他们的投资来说，最好的保护是强大的防御姿态。现在团结一致，他们准备将这一理念带到星空中，旨在保持边境稳固，缓解紧张局势。

我们不确定这个装置可能包含什么秘密。\n\n我们谨慎乐观地认为，在检查完它的结构后，我们将能够进一步推进我们的社会进步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:602`

### English

We are uncertain what secrets this device may contain.\n\nWe are cautiously optimistic that we will be able to advance our society further after examining its construction.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:731`

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:605`

### English

Under assault from an unknown force, the entities currently inhabiting the center of the galaxy have requested our assistance. Should they fall, we know not our fate.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:735`

## astral_situation.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶球体被毁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

Crystal Sphere Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:752`

## astral_situation.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

结束了。\n\n水晶球体已经被打碎成无数闪闪发光的碎屑。\n\n我们立即回收了数量丰富的珍贵材料。它的基本元素与我们以往所遇到的任何东西都不同。\n\n我们的人民正在庆祝这一保护我们家园安全的激进决定。让这样的装置留在我们的边境上是不负责任的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:622`

### English

It is done.\n\nThe Crystalline Sphere has been shattered into innumerable glittering grains.\n\nWe immediately harvested the bounty of valuable material. Its base elements are unlike anything we have ever encountered before.\n\nOur people are celebrating the bold decision to maintain the safety and security of our home. Allowing such a device to remain within our borders would have been irresponsible.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:753`

## astral_situation.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.5.name&lang=en)

### 中文

研究结束

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:623`

### English

The Study Is Over

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:755`

## astral_situation.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_situation.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一条消息。\n\n刻录在晶体内部的某个不可能存在的网络中，有一组指令。\n\n我们严格按照指令去做，成功地再造了蚀刻在晶体球周围所暗示的条件。一种精确的气体和星界丝缕的排列已经起到了“激活”这个球体的作用。\n\n随后，它突然消失了。取而代之的是一个我们从未见过的星界裂隙凝聚而成的景象。\n\n它的存在邀请我们去探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:624`

### English

It is a message.\n\nEncoded within an impossible network of crystal, there were a set of instructions.\n\nFollowed to the letter, we were able to recreate the conditions inscribed around the crystal sphere. A precise arrangement of gases and astral thread have worked to 'activate' the sphere.\n\nShortly afterwards, it simply disappeared. In its place, an Astral Rift unlike anything we have ever seen coalesced into view.\n\nIts existence invites exploration.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:756`

## astral_planes.15.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.15.name&lang=en)

### 中文

水晶球体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

The Crystal Sphere

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:758`

## astral_planes.15.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.15.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

一个奇怪的物体已经到达[Root.Capital.GetName]的轨道上。\n\n这个晶态球形物体让人想起我们自己的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!技术，它突然出现在没有任何已知轨迹的地方。\n\n由于与[Root.Capital.GetName]如此接近，这个球体可能对我们的人民构成重大威胁。\n\n另一方面，它很有可能包含有关维度旅行性质的珍贵线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

A strange object has arrived in orbit of [Root.Capital.GetName].\n\nA crystalline sphere reminiscent of our own §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! technology, it appeared without warning along no known trajectory.\n\nAt such close proximity to [Root.Capital.GetName], the sphere may represent a significant danger to our people.\n\nOn the other hand, it is also highly likely to contain valuable insights into the nature of dimensional travel.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:759`

## astral_planes.15.desc.win

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.15.desc.win&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经控制了[crystal_sphere_system.GetName]轨道上的一个奇怪物体。\n\n这是一个晶态球状物体，与我们开发的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!技术极其相似，该技术使我们能够探索其他维度。\n\n它可能对我们的帝国构成重大的未知危险。\n\n另一方面，我们相信它可能包含一些有价值的关于维度旅行的珍贵线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:627`

### English

We have taken control of a strange object in the orbit of [crystal_sphere_system.GetName].\n\nIt is a crystalline spherical object most similar to the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! technology we have developed that allows us to explore other dimensions.\n\nIt may present a significant unknown danger to our empire.\n\nOn the other hand, we believe it might contain some valuable insights into dimensional travel.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:760`

## astral_planes.15.desc.win_delayed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.15.desc.win_delayed&lang=en)

### 中文

鉴于我们最近对星界裂隙的研究，我们发现位于[crystal_sphere_system.GetName]的晶态球状物体与我们开发的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!技术有很多相似之处。\n\n它可能对我们的帝国构成重大未知危险。\n\n另一方面，我们相信它可能包含一些有关维度旅行的珍贵线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

In light of our recent research into Astral Rifts, we have discovered that the crystalline spherical object in [crystal_sphere_system.GetName] bares many similarities to the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! technology we have developed.\n\nIt may present a significant unknown danger to our empire.\n\nOn the other hand, we believe it might contain some valuable insights into dimensional travel.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:761`

## astral_planes.45.name

### 中文

$STRANGE_WORMHOLE$

### English

$STRANGE_WORMHOLE$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:847`

## astral_planes.45.desc.event_chain_owner

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.45.desc.event_chain_owner&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H[From.GetName]§!内附近的§H晶体裂隙§!处出现了一个异常的虫洞。\n\n我们只能推测这是无形异使打开的一个星门，为他们带来援军。我们检测到另一侧的恒星系已经从其他地方被拉入我们的位面。\n\n如果我们选择进入这个地方，我们应该准备我们最强大的舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

An anomalous wormhole has appeared nearby the §HCrystal Rift§!, in §H[From.GetName]§!.\n\nWe can only assume that it is a gateway opened by the Formless to bring them aid. We detect the system on the other side has been pulled into our dimension from somewhere else.\n\nWe should prepare our strongest fleets if we choose to enter this place.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:848`

## astral_planes.45.desc.locked

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.45.desc.locked&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一个异常的虫洞。无论我们的舰船试图靠近多近，都会被一股未知的力量推开。在§H[From.GetName]§!恒星系中发生了非常奇怪的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

We have discovered an anomalous wormhole. No matter how close our vessels attempt to proceed, we are being repelled by an unknown force. Something very odd is happening in the §H[From.GetName]§! system.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:849`

## astral_planes.45.desc.unlocked

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.45.desc.unlocked&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[From.GetName]§!发现了一个异常的虫洞。传感器显示它直接通往星系的中心。这怎么可能？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

We have discovered an anomalous wormhole in §H[From.GetName]§!. Sensors detect it is a direct pathway to the center of the galaxy. How is that possible?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:850`

## astral_planes.50.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.50.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$STRANGE_WORMHOLE$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:701`

### English

$STRANGE_WORMHOLE$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:852`

## astral_planes.50.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.50.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§H[crystal_sphere_system.GetName]§!恒星系中发现的异常虫洞正在剧烈波动。传感器检测到它是通往星系中心的直接通道。\n\n这怎么可能？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

The anomalous wormhole we discovered in the §H[crystal_sphere_system.GetName]§! system is fluctuating wildly. Sensors detect it is a direct pathway to the center of the galaxy.\n\nHow is that possible?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:853`

## astral_planes.3000

我们在哪？

### English

Where Are We?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:855`

## astral_planes.3000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个虫洞的另一边似乎把我们带到了……星系的中心？\n\n我们不应该存在于这里，更别说整个恒星系了。也许有一些维度操控在起作用吗？\n\n我们没有太多时间来调查，因为我们正在接收来自一个未知实体的传输信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

The other side of this wormhole seems to have taken us to... the center of the galaxy?\n\nIt should not be possible for us to exist here, let alone an entire system. Is there perhaps some dimensional manipulation at play?\n\nWe have little time to investigate, as we are receiving transmissions from an unknown entity.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:856`

## astral_planes.3005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是什么……你带来了什么傻瓜来帮助你，沙达衮？或者……你给我们提供了食物……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

what is this... what fools have you brought to aid you, shadagon? or perhaps... you have offered us food...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:858`

## astral_planes.3005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们被当作猎物，被诱惑到此地？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

Have we been lured here as prey?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:859`

## astral_planes.3015

### English

## astral_planes.3015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队已经击败了守卫这个裂隙的奇怪船只，但似乎它们并不孤单。\n\n我们收到了一段传输信号，来源于这个恒星系唯一行星旁边的建筑物内的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

Our fleets have defeated the strange ships guarding this rift, but it seems they were not alone.\n\nWe have received a transmission from beings inhabiting a structure nearby the lone planet in this system.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:862`

## astral_planes.3020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3020.desc&lang=en)

更多的即将到来……我们必须封闭这个裂缝……我们是唯一剩下的人……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

more are coming... we must seal this rift... we are all that remains...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:864`

finally... abdication... our chains are broken... behold our ascension... we will find what they have hidden...

## astral_planes.3070.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3070.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

被我们所知为无形异使的存在，已经被来自另一个位面的攻击者摧毁。位于$NAME_Formless_System$恒星系的传送门也被封印关闭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:718`

### English

The beings known as the Formless have been destroyed by attackers from another dimension. The portal into the $NAME_Formless_System$ system has sealed shut.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:875`

## astral_planes.3100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H在不断变化的状态下，这个实体的可见形态在这个现实和另一个现实之间转换。祂的声音共鸣如众多的和声，仿佛同时代表着几个存在。§!\n\n……完成了……封印已经设置完毕……我们的孩子再也找不到这个地方了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

§HIn a constant state of flux, this entity's visible form shifts between this reality and another. Their voice resonates as a harmonic multitude, as speaking on behalf of several beings at once.§!\n\n... it is done... the seal is set... never shall our children find this place again...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:877`

## astral_planes.3121.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3121.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

沙达衮的§Y$NAME_Formless_leader$§!……你怎么会在这里……\n\n你将成为一顿盛宴……\n\n来吧，与我们一同碰撞……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

§Y$NAME_Formless_leader$§! of shadagon... how is it you are here...\n\nyou will be a great feast...\n\ncome and break against us...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:925`

## astral_planes.3122.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3122.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还没有结束……沙达衮的§Y$NAME_Formless_leader$§!……\n\n你救了你的亲族……但愚蠢地追求……傲慢……\n\n见证愤怒……然后是遗忘……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

we are not finished... §Y$NAME_Formless_leader$§! of shadagon...\n\nyou have saved your kin... but foolishly pursue... hubris...\n\nwitness fury... then oblivion...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:926`

## astral_planes.3123.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3123.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

建筑师……沙达衮的§Y$NAME_Formless_leader$§!……\n\n我们将吞食你……吸收你的思维……\n\n王座的秘密……你提供的伟大礼物……\n\n现在加入我们……我们将尊重你……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

the architect... §Y$NAME_Formless_leader$§! of shadagon...\n\nwe will consume you...  incorporate your mind...\n\nthe secrets of the throne... a great gift you offer...\n\njoin us now... we will honor you...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:927`

## formless_leader_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aformless_leader_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无形异使的最后一位领袖之一，这个高维实体指挥舰队的时间比大多数物种的寿命还要长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

One of the last leaders of the Formless, this extra-dimensional entity has commanded fleets for longer than the lifetime of most species.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:934`

## astral_planes.3155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们针对无形异使的侵略行动，已经导致[this.GetName]放弃将舰队委任给我们，这些舰队重新加入他们的队列。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

Our aggressive campaign against the Formless has caused [this.GetName] to abandon their assigned fleets, and rejoin their ranks.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无形者被摧毁

### English

Formless Destroyed

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:941`

## astral_planes.3165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已成功消灭了在[event_target:formless_system.GetName]中剩下的最后一批无形异使。\n\n剩下的只有一个神秘的装置。一个人只需将自己的[this.species.GetHandNamePlural]放在上面，就能激活它神奇的再生特性。该装置作为一种通道进入星界位面，其中有大量的超维材料经由它自由流动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:774`

### English

We have successfully exterminated the last of the remaining Formless in [event_target:formless_system.GetName].\n\nAll that remains is a mysterious device. One need simply place their [this.species.GetHandNamePlural] upon it to activate its miraculous regenerative properties. The device functions as a sort of conduit into the astral planes, with incredible quantities of extra-dimensional material flowing freely throughout it.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:942`

## astral_planes.3170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.3170.desc&lang=en)

though we know you...\nit is not our time to meet...

### 中文

被称为无形异使的存在所拥有的最后堡垒。在他们的庇护下，其他维度的物种会逐渐于此定居下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:777`

## preset_luminarium_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreset_luminarium_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曙光境是永恒王座的所有者所创造的附属国类型。无形异使的跨次元帝国业已灰飞烟灭，拥有了王座便可驱使他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

A Luminarium is a subject type created by the owner of the Eternal Throne. Those who claim this seat hold influence over the Formless, whose dimension-spanning empire has since fallen into ruin.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:951`

### English

$pc_astral_scar$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:998`

## anomaly_astral_scar_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_astral_scar_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一个时空中的异常裂缝。我们越靠近，已知的物理相对论定律越不适用。\n\n研究这一现象很可能将推动我们对宇宙机制的科学理解提升到一个更高的水平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:822`

### English

We have discovered an anomalous rift in space and time. The closer we get, the less the known laws of physical relativity hold purchase.\n\nStudying this phenomenon could very well push our scientific understanding of the mechanics of the universe to a higher level.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:999`

## astral_planes.6000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$pc_astral_scar$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:823`

### English

$pc_astral_scar$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1000`

## astral_planes.6000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经完成了对位于§Y[FromFrom.System.GetName]§!的§Y$pc_astral_scar$§!的分析。\n\n它正在从其他地方向我们的宇宙泄漏物质。裂缝周围的物质并不是守恒的——至少从我们的测量方法上来说不是的。尽管处于相对微小的规模，但裂缝似乎增加了我们宇宙的总物质量。\n\n研究这一现象应该能更好地理解这种无法解释的能源来源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:824`

### English

We have completed our analysis of the §Y$pc_astral_scar$§! in §Y[FromFrom.System.GetName]§!.\n\nIt is leaking material into our universe from... somewhere else. Matter is not being conserved around the rift - at least not in any way that we can measure. Though on a relatively minute scale, somehow the rift is increasing the overall mass of our universe.\n\nResearching this phenomenon should provide a greater understanding of this unaccounted for source of energy.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1001`

## ASTRAL_SCAR_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AASTRAL_SCAR_PROJECT&lang=en)

$pc_astral_scar$

### English

$pc_astral_scar$

## ASTRAL_SCAR_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AASTRAL_SCAR_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一处时空的异常裂缝。研究它的独特性质应该能够提供我们有价值的科学启发。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:826`

### English

We have discovered an anomalous rift in space and time. Researching its unique properties should provide valuable scientific insight.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1003`

## astral_planes.6001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$pc_astral_scar$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:827`

### English

$pc_astral_scar$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1004`

## astral_planes.6001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经确认了在§Y[FromFromFrom.System.GetName]§!恒星系中存在一个我们宇宙之外的宇宙。\n\n通过裂缝，它的物质位面对我们自己的宇宙施加了巨大的压力，所造成的结果是有一小部分但稳定的物质流动。这一情况的长期效应我们尚未被理解，但在此期间，我们应继续分析其宇宙流出物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:828`

### English

We have confirmed the existence of another universe beyond our own in the §Y[FromFromFrom.System.GetName]§! system.\n\nThrough the rift, its physical dimensions are placing immense pressure on our own universe, the result of which is a small though steady flow of material. The long-term effects are yet to be understood, but in the meantime we should continue analyzing its cosmic effluence.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1005`

## astral_planes.6001.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6001.a&lang=en)

### 中文

另一边存在着怎样的奇景呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:829`

### English

What wonders might lie beyond?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1006`

不稳定$pc_astral_scar$

### English

$pc_astral_scar$ Instability

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1008`

## ASTRAL_SCAR_INSTABILITY_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AASTRAL_SCAR_INSTABILITY_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

不稳定的引力异常正在从[From.System.GetName]的§Y$pc_astral_scar$§!中泄漏出来，显现为缓慢移动的奇点在附近空间中传播，然后剧烈地内爆。穿越这个恒星系的舰船可以避开它们，但必须小心谨慎地行驶。\n\n进一步调查这种情况，可能能够发现稳定维度裂口的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:831`

### English

Unstable gravitational anomalies are leaking out of the §Y$pc_astral_scar$§! in [From.System.GetName], manifesting as slow-moving singularities that travel throughout nearby space before violently imploding. Ships passing through this system can avoid them, but must travel more carefully to do so.\n\nInvestigating this situation further could reveal a way to restabilize the dimensional opening.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1009`

### English

## astral_planes.6006.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6006.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缝补时空

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:833`

### English

A Stitch in Time (and Space)

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1011`

## astral_planes.6006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过仔细分析，我们确认[From.System.GetName]中的§Y$pc_astral_scar$§!异常不稳定，需要立即干预以防止该恒星系进一步坍缩为引力奇点。\n\n可以考虑将大量喷射而出的§Y$astral_threads$§!进行重定向、重新利用，以便“缝合”裂痕。这将修复时空的撕裂，并可能留下大量的星界物质。\n\n或者，我们可以投射大量军火来扩大这处裂痕。我们预测，这么做将有助于我们探索伤痕另一边可能存在的事物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:834`

### English

After careful analysis, we've confirmed that the §Y$pc_astral_scar$§! in [From.System.GetName] is dangerously unstable and requires immediate intervention to prevent further gravitational singularities forming in this system.\n\nThe powerful ejection of §Y$astral_threads$§! could be redirected and repurposed in order to 'stitch' the scar shut. This would repair the tear in space-time and likely leave behind a significant amount of astral material.\n\nAlternatively, we could deliver a heavy payload of ordinance in an effort to widen the scar. We speculate that this would also allow for further exploration of what may be on the other side.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1012`

### English
