## astral_planes.6007.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6007.name&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙之戕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:837`

### English

Cosmic Wound

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1015`

## astral_planes.6007.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6007.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过对其撕裂织构的精细操控，我们成功地完全封印了位于[From.System.GetName]的§Y$pc_astral_scar$§!。引力异常自那时起停止，我们在整个过程中回收了大量的§Y$astral_threads$§!。\n\n无论裂缝另一边的现实如何，都不再对我们构成威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:838`

### English

After careful manipulation of its torn fabric, we've managed to fully seal the §Y$pc_astral_scar$§! in [From.System.GetName]. Gravitational anomalies have since stopped, and we've recovered a significant quantity of §Y$astral_threads$§! in the process.\n\nWhatever reality was on the other side will no longer pose a danger to our own.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1016`

## astral_planes.6008.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6008.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_planes.6007.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:839`

### English

$astral_planes.6007.name$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1017`

## astral_planes.6008.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6008.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们投送到到[From.System.GetName]的§Y$pc_astral_scar$§!核心的军火扩大了时空的折叠。这里曾经只是宇宙织物中的一个小撕裂口，现在成为了一个更大的裂隙，能够让我们进入探索。\n\n由此带来的稳定性使引力异常减弱，我们准备去发现隐藏在其中的事物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:840`

### English

The ordinance we sent into the core of the §Y$pc_astral_scar$§! in [From.System.GetName] has widened the folds in space and time. Where there was once a small tear in the cosmic fabric, there is now a larger rift that we are capable of exploring.\n\nThe resulting stability has caused the gravitational anomalies to subside, and we are prepared to discover what lies beyond.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1018`

## astral_planes.6009.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6009.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星界坍缩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:841`

### English

Astral Collapse

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1019`

## astral_planes.6009.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6009.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

位于[FromFromFromFrom.System.GetName]的§Y$pc_astral_scar$§!已经朝内坍缩了，并朝周边恒星系抛射出了大量的$astral_threads$。这里的宇宙织构曾经出现过一处小小的撕裂，但如今已经是虚无的太空。\n\n无论裂缝另一边的现实如何，都不再对我们构成威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:842`

### English

The §Y$pc_astral_scar$§! in [FromFromFromFrom.System.GetName] has collapsed inwardly, ejecting a significant amount of $astral_threads$ into the nearby system. Where there was once a small tear in the cosmic fabric, there is now empty space.\n\nWhatever reality was on the other side will no longer pose a danger to our own.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1020`

## GREATEST_SHOW_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGREATEST_SHOW_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

探测到光亮物体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:843`

### English

Bright Object Detected

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1022`

## anomaly_greatest_show_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_greatest_show_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.System.GetName]§!的一颗小行星内我们探测到了一个光亮四溢的物体。\n\n由高度先进材料构建，它正在所有频率上广播稳定的无线电波。我们的扫描仪显示其内部没有含有爆炸性或挥发性化合物。看起来我们可以安全接近并进一步开展研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:844`

### English

A brightly lit object has been detected on an asteroid in §Y[from.System.GetName]§!.\n\nConstructed of highly advanced materials, it is broadcasting a steady stream of radio waves on all frequencies. Our scanners indicate no explosive or volatile compounds contained within. It seems safe to approach and study further.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1023`

## astral_planes.6010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

多重宇宙的最佳秀场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:845`

### English

The Greatest Show in the Multiverse

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1024`

## astral_planes.6010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在这颗小行星上发现的发光物体是一则广告。\n\n这个巨大的展示装置由内部占据小行星的机械基础设施支持。小行星本身在空间中的某一点上异常地悬浮着，不受任何附近物体的引力影响。\n\n广告本身是关于它所称之为“多重宇宙的最佳秀场”的内容。有一个终端可以接受能量币，使我们直接前往这个景点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:846`

### English

The brightly lit object we discovered on this asteroid is an advertisement.\n\nThis massive display is supported by a mechanical infrastructure occupying the inside of the asteroid it is mounted on. The asteroid itself is anomalously suspended at one point in space, unaffected by the gravitational pull of any nearby object.\n\nThe advertisement itself is in reference to what it calls "The Greatest Show in the Multiverse".There is a terminal accepting energy credits for direct transport to the attraction.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1025`

## astral_planes.6010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

付钱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

Pay the fee.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1026`

### English

Repurpose the sign for our own use.

### English

## astral_planes.6011.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6011.name&lang=en)

裂隙显现

### English

A Rift Appears

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1029`

## astral_planes.6011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在处理完我们的支付款项后，小行星内部的机械开始嗡嗡作响。一股刺眼的蓝光向外射出，精确地分开了时空的织构。当它运转结束时，小行星就消失了，而一道星界裂隙出现在了原地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:851`

### English

After processing our payment, the machinery within the asteroid whirred into life. A stream of piercing blue light shot outward, meticulously parting the fabric of space and time. Once its work was finished, the asteroid simply vanished, and an Astral Rift was left in its stead.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1030`

## astral_planes.6011.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6011.a&lang=en)

### 中文

印象深刻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:852`

### English

Impressive.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1031`

## astral_planes.6012.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6012.name&lang=en)

### 中文

高效宣发

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

Effective Advertising

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1032`

## astral_planes.6012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们用深受认可的本地企业代替了[fromfrom.GetName]的古怪广告商。支付终端被仔细拆解后，我们在里面发现了一小批能量币。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:854`

### English

We have replaced the anomalous advertisement with an endorsement for our own local businesses at [fromfrom.GetName]. The payment terminal was carefully dismantled, revealing a small cache of energy credits within.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1033`

## astral_planes.6012.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6012.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真会做生意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

How enterprising.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1034`

## astral_planes.6013.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6013.name&lang=en)

### 中文

复杂的标识系统

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

Complex Signage

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1035`

## astral_planes.6013.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6013.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星内部机械中所采纳的技术比我们最初预料的要先进。因此，我们的回收工作给我们带来了相当可观的价值。我们发现了各种稀有材料——其中包括数量惊人的§Y$astral_threads$§!。这颗中空的小行星一定是被设计成能够以某种方式操纵空间和时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

The technology within the asteroid's internal mechanisms were more advanced than initially suspected. As such, its salvage has rewarded us with considerable value. We have uncovered a variety of rare materials - including an anomalous quantity of §Y$astral_threads$§!. The hollowed asteroid must have been designed to manipulate space and time in some fashion.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1036`

## astral_planes.6013.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6013.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们得好好利用这一切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

We shall put these to good use.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1037`

## BLACK_HOLE_INSTABLE_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABLACK_HOLE_INSTABLE_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

不稳定$sc_black_hole$

### English

$sc_black_hole$ Instability

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1039`

## anomaly_black_hole_instable_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_black_hole_instable_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的扫描仪检测到了[from.System.GetName]恒星系中黑洞的物理特性与预期相比存在显著偏差。它的引力不可预测的，这导致空间中一个点到另一个点在时间上表现出显著的不一致性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

Our scanners have detected significant deviations from the expected physical properties of the black hole in the [from.System.GetName] system. Its gravitational forces are unpredictable, resulting in noticeable temporal inconsistencies from one point in space to another.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1040`

## astral_planes.6020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不稳定的黑洞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

Black Hole Instability

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1041`

## astral_planes.6020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

某些由[from.System.GetName]的黑洞释放的粒子实际上是§Y$astral_threads$§!。这意味着在某个时间点，星界细缝被拉入了黑洞的奇点，然后现在正在缓慢地被喷射出来。这样的事件以前被认为是不可能的。\n\n或许，存在某种方式将高度聚集的§Y$astral_threads$§!羽流导入奇点之中，从而让星界细缝被喷射出来，使这个恒星系的空间重新稳定。\n\n然而，若是不采取进一步行动，我们可以继续从安全距离采集这些宇宙织物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:862`

### English

Some of the particles being released by the black hole in [from.System.GetName] are, in fact, §Y$astral_threads$§!. This means that at some point in time an Astral Scar was pulled into its singularity and is now slowly being ejected. Such an event was previously thought impossible.\n\nThere may be a way to eject the scar and restabilize this system's space by directing a highly concentrated stream of §Y$astral_threads$§! into the singularity.\n\nHowever, taking no further action would allow us to continue harvesting the cosmic fabric from a safe distance.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1042`

## astral_planes.6020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6020.a&lang=en)

往奇点注入星界物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:863`

### English

Inject astral material into the singularity.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1043`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6020.b&lang=en)

### English

## astral_planes.6021.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6021.name&lang=en)

裂隙显现

### English

A Rift Appears

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1045`

## astral_planes.6021.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6021.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将§Y$astral_threads$§!注入奇点立马引起了剧烈的反应，由于黑洞的引力，大量的超维粒子喷涌而出。这些粒子已经聚集成一个悬浮在其引力井中的$astral_rift$。\n\n这个天体异常点之前是如何被吸入奇点，我们仍然不了解，但现在我们至少可以调查一下裂隙的另一边有什么存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:866`

### English

The injection of §Y$astral_threads$§! into the singularity caused an immediate and violent reaction, as a flood of extra-dimensional particles poured forth from the gravitational pull of the black hole. These particles have coalesced into an $astral_rift$ that is now suspended within its gravity well.\n\nHow this celestial anomaly was ever sucked into the singularity in the first place remains unknown, but now we can at least investigate what lies on the other side.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1046`

## astral_planes.6022.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6022.name&lang=en)

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6022.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[from.System.GetName]的黑洞将先前被其奇点吸收的$pc_astral_scar$抛射了出来，随之而来的还有大量被束缚的星界物质。\n\n黑洞的引力现在变得正常了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:868`

### English

The black hole in [from.System.GetName] has ejected the $pc_astral_scar$ that was temporarily absorbed into its singularity, along with a significant amount of trapped astral material.\n\nThe gravitational forces of the black hole have since normalized.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1048`

，其位于行星南半球的可观测天空中。人们激烈地推测它的起源，以及它蕴含着怎样的危险或机遇。\n\n来自[owner.GetName]各地的业余天文爱好者聚集到这颗行星，亲眼见证这番绝景。

visible in the sky from the planet's southern hemisphere. Rampant speculation exists about its origins, as well as what dangers or opportunities it might present.\n\nAmateur astronomers from across the [owner.GetName] have flocked to the planet to see it for themselves.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6035.desc.common_3&lang=en)

### 中文

通往的是一个发条宇宙的中心，那里有一个机械神，他会给时间表上发条，以在无尽的世界中保持平衡。\n\n与之相反，还有人说他们的幻境中出现了一颗黑色的恒星，它拥有启蒙的关键。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:881`

### English

leads to the center of a clockwork galaxy, where a mechanical god winds a timepiece to maintain balance across infinite worlds.\n\nIn contrast, others say their visions have revealed a black star that holds the key to enlightenment.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1063`

### 中文

人群之间的割裂在[this.GetName]上日益加剧。殖民者报告的奇怪幻境引发了激烈的辩论，并最终演变为暴力事件。当地领导人呼吁发表一份官方的声明。\n\n尽管可能看起来荒谬，我们被要求为机械神或黑星的存在提供可信说明，作为

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:892`

### English

The schism between the population on [this.GetName] is worsening. The strange visions reported by the colonists have triggered fierce debates that have finally led to outbreaks of violence. Local leaders are calling for an official proclamation.\n\nAs outlandish as it may seem, we are being asked to lend credibility to either the existence of a mechanical god or a black star beyond their

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1075`

## astral_planes.6045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[this.GetName]上发生了灾难！\n\n人们所经历的相互冲突的视景导致了大规模的动荡。竞相敌对的团体已经拿起了武器，局部的小规模冲突导致了全球范围内的暴力冲突。\n\n这种情况不能继续下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

Disaster on [this.GetName]!\n\nThe contrasting visions experienced by the population have resulted in large-scale unrest. The competing groups have taken up arms, with local skirmishes leading to violent clashes all across the planet.\n\nThis can not be allowed to continue.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1083`

所有关于[this.GetName]上的奇怪幻境的公开讨论已被禁止。任何被发现谈论这个禁忌话题的人都会受到严厉的惩罚，这就阻止了公开冲突的发生。\n\n许多殖民者已经转而追求新的热点，但一小部分硬核的业余天文学家仍然享受着这个壮观景象，

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:915`

Severe punishments for anyone caught broaching the forbidden topic have discouraged open conflict.\n\nMany colonists have moved on to new pursuits, but a core group of amateur astronomers continue to enjoy the sheer spectacle of the

## astral_planes.6060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.GetName]§!的地表布满了有缺陷的军火，每次爆炸都会在行星上切割出一块块的碎片。\n\n我们对这些武器十分陌生，其本身又是极其易爆的。此外，爆炸产生的图案表明了整个行星上埋藏这一个起爆网络。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:923`

### English

The surface of §Y[from.GetName]§! is riddled with defective munitions, each carving out chunks of the planet as it detonates.\n\nThe weapons themselves are unfamiliar and extremely volatile. Furthermore, the pattern of the explosive discharges indicates a network of buried launch sites across the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1110`

## astral_planes.6065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步的分析表明，这颗行星曾经被用作巨大的武器储存库。在静止了数千年之后，个别武器现在开始出现故障，将整个行星变成了一个不稳定的战场。\n\n我们有能力以受控的方式触发所有剩余的弹药。或者，我们可以尝试提取一些武器并在它们爆炸之前将其停用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:928`

### English

Further analysis indicates that this planet was once used as an enormous weapons cache. Having sat inactive for thousands of years, individual weapons are now beginning to malfunction, turning the entire planet into an unstable battlefield.\n\nWe have the capacity to trigger all remaining munitions in a controlled manner. Alternatively, we could attempt to extract some of the weapons and deactivate them before they explode.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1116`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6065.a&lang=en)

### English

### English

## astral_planes.6070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

裂隙显现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:932`

### English

A Rift Appears

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1121`

## astral_planes.6070.desc.common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6070.desc.common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

在[from.GetName]周围部署了一系列精心设计的炸药之后，我们触发了链式反应，彻底摧毁了这颗行星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:933`

### English

After deploying a carefully engineered set of charges around [from.GetName], we triggered a chain reaction that destroyed the planet entirely.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1122`

## astral_planes.6070.desc.common_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6070.desc.common_2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们通过理论推导得出，这个裂隙的产生是因为一场极其强大的、能够产生奇点的爆炸，这是由武器储藏库中的某一件武器产生的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:934`

### English

We theorize that the opening of this rift was caused by a uniquely powerful singularity-generating explosive stored in one of the many weapon caches.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1123`

## astral_planes.6070.desc.no_rift

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6070.desc.no_rift&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_planes.6070.desc.common_1$\n\n由此引发的爆炸十分强大，不仅摧毁了行星，还在时空织构中撕扯出了一处裂口，导致了一个我们现在称之为§Y$astral_rift$§!的形成。\n\n$astral_planes.6070.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:935`

### English

$astral_planes.6070.desc.common_1$\n\nThe resulting explosion was so strong that it not only destroyed the planet, but also tore a hole in the fabric of space-time, resulting in the formation of what we are calling an §Y$astral_rift$§!.\n\n$astral_planes.6070.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1124`

## astral_planes.6070.desc.rift

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6070.desc.rift&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_planes.6070.desc.common_1$\n\n由此引发的爆炸十分强大，不仅摧毁了行星，还产生了一道新的§Y$astral_rift$§!。\n\n$astral_planes.6070.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:936`

### English

$astral_planes.6070.desc.common_1$\n\nThe resulting explosion was so strong that it not only destroyed the planet, but resulted in the formation of a new §Y$astral_rift$§!.\n\n$astral_planes.6070.desc.common_2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1125`

## astral_planes.6075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

成功提取武器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:937`

### English

Successful Weapon Extraction

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1127`

## astral_planes.6075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能够为存储在行星表面的武器建立一个提取管线，将处于激活状态的军火变为惰性状态，然后准备收集和分析它们。\n\n在这个行星上建立一个永久殖民地将使我们能够更高效地利用剩余的储藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:938`

### English

We were able to create an extraction pipeline for the weapons stored within the planet's surface, rendering active munitions inert before preparing them for collection and analysis.\n\nEstablishing a permanent colony on this planet would allow us to take advantage of the remaining cache more efficiently.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1128`

## astral_planes.6076.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6076.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现奇点军火

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:939`

### English

Singularity Munition Discovered

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1130`

## astral_planes.6076.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6076.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

位于[from.GetName]的研究人员在行星上废弃的武器库中发现了一枚受损的奇点生成导弹。这种武器似乎被设计成能够通过暴力方式创建一个引力坍缩，有可能撕裂时空本身。\n\n不幸的是，这件武器已经损坏到无法修复的程度，也超出了我们的复制能力。然而，我们可能能够将其重新用于研究类似的爆炸性武器。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:940`

### English

Researchers studying [from.GetName] have discovered a damaged singularity-generating missile within the planet's abandoned weapon caches. This weapon was seemingly designed to create a violent gravitational implosion, with the potential to rend a hole in space-time itself.\n\nUnfortunately, this armament is damaged beyond repair and beyond our capabilities to replicate. However, we may be able to repurpose it as part of our research into similar explosive weapons.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1131`

## astral_planes.6076.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6076.a&lang=en)

### English

## astral_planes.6080.desc.common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6080.desc.common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

在尝试为[from.GetName]上被废弃的武器建立一个提取管线时，[this.GetName]被一个失灵的爆炸装置摧毁了。船上的所有人都丧生了，包括[leader.GetName]。\n\n船的爆炸进一步触发了武器储藏网络中的一系列不稳定的连锁反应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:943`

### English

While attempting to create an extraction pipeline for the abandoned weapons on [from.GetName], [this.GetName] was destroyed by a malfunctioning explosive. All aboard the vessel were lost, including [leader.GetName].\n\nThe ship's explosion further triggered a volatile chain reaction across the network of weapon caches.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1135`

## astral_planes.6085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

连锁反应

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:947`

### English

Chain Reaction

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1140`

## astral_planes.6085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[from.GetName]上的不稳定武器由于自发爆炸引发了一连串的连锁反应，导致该行星完全破裂。\n\n在残骸中，我们发现了大量的§Y$volatile_motes$§!。这种资源一定是存储在地表下的武器的关键组成部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:948`

### English

The spontaneous explosions of unstable weapons on [from.GetName] culminated in a chain reaction that caused the planet to completely break apart.\n\nAmong the debris, we have detected a significant concentration of §Y$volatile_motes$§!. This resource must have formed a key component of the weapons stored beneath the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1141`

## TUTORIAL_ASTRAL_ACTIONS_VIEW_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ASTRAL_ACTIONS_VIEW_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:997`

### English

Tutorial: Astral Actions

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1198`

## TUTORIAL_ASTRAL_ACTIONS_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ASTRAL_ACTIONS_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

星界行动界面允许我们使用$ASTRAL_THREADS_ICON$来产生各种全国范围内的效果。我们探索的§S星界裂隙§!越多，我们就能解锁更多行动。有些行动可以随时间重复使用，而其他行动只能使用一次以解锁新的能力或技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:998`

### English

The Astral Actions screen allows us to spend our $ASTRAL_THREADS_ICON$ on various empire-wide effects. The more §SAstral Rifts§! we explore, the more actions we will unlock. Some actions are repeatable over time, while others can be used only once to unlock new abilities or technologies.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1199`

## TUTORIAL_ASTRAL_RIFT_VIEW_TITLE

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ASTRAL_RIFT_VIEW_TITLE&lang=en)

### 中文

星界裂隙界面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:999`

### English

Astral Rift View

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1201`

## TUTORIAL_ASTRAL_RIFT_VIEW_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATUTORIAL_ASTRAL_RIFT_VIEW_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

星界裂隙窗口显示当前选择的§S星界裂隙§!的详细信息。在项目进展的每个阶段，你需要根据提出给科学官下达命令，这将导致不同的、有时意想不到的结果。大多数征服星界裂隙的探险都有多条可供选择的路径。\n\n为了揭开某个星界裂隙的秘密，科学官必须取得§S突破§!，以在星界裂隙的每个阶段取得进展。具有相关§S特质和技能水平更高§!的科学官可以更快地抵达下一个阶段，而所选路径的难度评级更高将减慢进展速度。任何长时间调查裂隙的科学官都将积累§S线索§!，这将增加突破的可能性。\n\n在探索§S星界裂隙§!时，科学官被束缚在另一个维度中，直到探险完成才能解除其职务。由于以这种方式被困在时间和空间之外，他们不会因为年老而死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1000`

### English

The Astral Rift window shows details of the currently selected §SAstral Rift§!. At each stage of progress, you may be prompted with orders to give our Scientists that will lead to varying, sometimes unexpected results. Most expeditions beyond Astral Rifts have multiple paths to follow.\n\nTo unravel the secrets of a Rift, a Scientist must make a §SBreakthrough§! to advance through each of the Rift's stages. Scientists with relevant §STraits and higher Skill§! speed the process of advancing to the next stage, while a higher Difficulty rating of a path choice will slow it. Any Scientist who investigates a Rift for long enough will amass §SClues§! which will make a Breakthrough more likely.\n\nWhile exploring an §SAstral Rift§!, Scientists are tethered into another dimension and cannot be removed until the exploration is complete. As a consequence of being trapped between time and space in this fashion, they cannot die from old age.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1202`

## astral_planes.6100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6100.name&lang=en)

### English

## astral_planes.6100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

与[FromFromFrom.GetName]的裂隙探球一同从星界裂隙出现的，是一幅引起了我们注意的异常景象：探球的尾迹中跟随着一个休眠的逃生舱。很快，我们就收到了一个简单的求救信号，并附有实时视频。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1003`

### English

Emerging from the Astral Rift alongside the [FromFromFrom.GetName]'s Rift Sphere, an unexpected sight catches our attention: a dormant escape pod trailing in the Sphere's wake. Soon, we receive a simple distress signal, accompanied by a live video feed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1207`

## Mercedes_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMercedes_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这位快乐的百岁老布洛葛对外星文化，尤其是人类充满热情。她勇敢地穿越了星际裂隙来到这里，任务是：交朋友。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1007`

### English

A centenarian with a passion for alien cultures, especially Humans, this cheerful Blorg braved an Astral Rift to get here. Their mission: making Friends.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1212`

## mercedes_catch_phrase

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amercedes_catch_phrase&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以交个朋友吗?

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1008`

### English

Can we be Friends?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1213`

## astral_planes.6105.desc.reg

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6105.desc.reg&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这只令人厌恶的外星人站在我们面前，茫然地沉默着，全身上下只有菌毛在像波浪一样有节奏地起伏着。这只生物突然回过神来，挥动触角自我介绍道:§!\n\n啊？怎么？这是哪？抱歉。我叫梅赛德斯·罗梅罗，是布洛葛合众国的探险家和外交官。你听说过人类吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1009`

### English

§HThe repugnant alien stands before us in dazed silence, its only motion that of its pileus, undulating in a wave like rythm. Abruptly regaining composure, with a flourish of tentacles the creature introduces itself: §!\n\nWhat- Whe- Where am I? I am sorry. My name is Mercedes Romero, explorer and diplomat of the Blorg Commmonality. Do you know of the Humans?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1214`

## astral_planes.6105.desc.human

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6105.desc.human&lang=en)

### 中文

§H这只令人厌恶的外星人站在我们面前，茫然地沉默着，全身上下只有菌毛在像波浪一样有节奏地起伏着。在注意到我们之后，她几乎要猛扑上来，然后又控制住了自己。§!\n\n你们、你们是人类！？§H*尖叫*§!我——我太抱歉了，从我还是一只萌孢的时候就对你们的文化超级感兴趣！\n\n§H这只外星人伸出了一只触手，令人尴尬地尝试模仿握手动作。§!\n\n我叫梅赛德斯·罗梅罗，很高兴见到你们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1010`

### English

§HThe repugnant alien stands before us in dazed silence, its only motion that of its pileus, undulating in a wave like rythm. Upon registering us, it makes a lunging movement before restraining itself.§!\n\nY- You- You are Humans!? §H*Squeee*§! I- I am sorry, I have been fascinated with your culture ever since I was a little sporeling!\n\n§HThe alien extends a tentacle in an awkward attempt to mimic a handshake.§!\n\nMy name is Mercedes Romero and it is my pleasure to meet you.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1215`

## astral_planes.6105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6105.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你记得些什么吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1011`

### English

What do you remember?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1216`

## astral_planes.6105.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6105.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

我最后的记忆是在心宿二星系。当时我和一群朋友在一起进行舰队行动，突然看到了一束强光。那之后就什么都不记得了。我是坐着逃生舱来的。孤独且无友。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1012`

### English

My last memory is of the Antares System. I was engaged in a fleet action with a group of Friends when I saw a bright light. After that there is nothing. I arrived here in my escape pod. Alone and Friendless.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1217`

## astral_planes.6105.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6105.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你为什么对人类如此感兴趣？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1013`

### English

Why are you so fascinated with Humans?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1218`

## astral_planes.6105.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6105.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

噢，人类，最美妙的生物。我们布洛葛已经关注了他们几个世纪。其实，我们文化的大部分都是基于对人类生活的观察。色彩！戏剧！友谊！他们教会我们生活远不只是生存，甚至还可以交朋友！这个概念真是绝妙，简直可以说是奇迹！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1014`

### English

Oh Humans are the most wondrous of creatures. We Blorg have been observing them for centuries. In fact, much of our culture is based on what we have observed of Human life. The color! The drama! The Friendship! They have taught us that life is about more than simple survival. You can also make Friends! What a wonderful concept. What a marvel!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1219`

## astral_planes.6105.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6105.c&lang=en)

### 中文

你能为我们做什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1015`

### English

What can you do for us?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1220`

## astral_planes.6105.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_planes.6105.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

这问题可真不友好，但我有求必应！当然，我有着上百年的驾驶科研船的经验，但我真正醉心的是跨星系关系。把我当成朋友，我可以帮你们交上更多的朋友。拜托了，如果你们感兴趣的话，务必让我代表你们的外交利益！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_planes_l_simp_chinese.yml:1016`

### English

Not a very Friendly question, but if service is required, then I stand ready! Of course, I have centuries of experience captaining research vessels, but my true passion is intergalactic relations. By being your Friend I can help you to make other Friends in the process. Please, if you will have me, let me represent your diplomatic interests!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_planes_l_english.yml:1221`
